Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any graphs module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 342. The projective resolution has length at most 74 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_342(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 342. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^74(S, F) is trivial. QED.
